# Cyclone UI (Light High Contrast) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInputField results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Tabs, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup, RadioGroupField and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Color

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color.accent.base` | `color` | `var(--color-neutral-14)` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. |
| `color.accent.base-active` | `color` | `#000000` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (active, light base primitive) |
| `color.accent.base-disabled` | `color` | `#000000` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (disabled, 80% saturation) |
| `color.accent.base-hover` | `color` | `#000000` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (hover, 30% brighter) |
| `color.accent.base-inactive` | `color` | `#000000` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (inactive, 40% darker) |
| `color.accent.brand` | `color` | `#00bcad` | Brand accent (teal) used by the `brand` theme. Use it for primary calls to action and brand moments, and sparingly elsewhere so it keeps its weight. Also drives text selection and is the default theme for Message. |
| `color.accent.brand-active` | `color` | `#00554f` | Brand accent (teal) used by the `brand` theme. Use it for primary calls to action and brand moments, and sparingly elsewhere so it keeps its weight. Also drives text selection and is the default theme for Message. (active, 19% darker) |
| `color.accent.brand-disabled` | `color` | `#35c4b5` | Brand accent (teal) used by the `brand` theme. Use it for primary calls to action and brand moments, and sparingly elsewhere so it keeps its weight. Also drives text selection and is the default theme for Message. (disabled, 80% saturation) |
| `color.accent.brand-hover` | `color` | `#004a44` | Brand accent (teal) used by the `brand` theme. Use it for primary calls to action and brand moments, and sparingly elsewhere so it keeps its weight. Also drives text selection and is the default theme for Message. (hover, 23% darker) |
| `color.accent.brand-inactive` | `color` | `#5cfbeb` | Brand accent (teal) used by the `brand` theme. Use it for primary calls to action and brand moments, and sparingly elsewhere so it keeps its weight. Also drives text selection and is the default theme for Message. (inactive, 20% brighter) |
| `color.accent.danger` | `color` | `var(--color-red-7)` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. |
| `color.accent.danger-active` | `color` | `#340013` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. (active, 19% darker) |
| `color.accent.danger-disabled` | `color` | `#7b182d` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. (disabled, 80% saturation) |
| `color.accent.danger-hover` | `color` | `#2d000f` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. (hover, 23% darker) |
| `color.accent.danger-inactive` | `color` | `#bc2445` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. (inactive, 20% brighter) |
| `color.accent.discovery` | `color` | `var(--color-purple-7)` | Discovery accent used by the `discovery` theme. Use it to highlight new features, onboarding and help content. Applied by `help` Messages. |
| `color.accent.discovery-active` | `color` | `#2d136b` | Discovery accent used by the `discovery` theme. Use it to highlight new features, onboarding and help content. Applied by `help` Messages. (active, 19% darker) |
| `color.accent.discovery-disabled` | `color` | `#48328a` | Discovery accent used by the `discovery` theme. Use it to highlight new features, onboarding and help content. Applied by `help` Messages. (disabled, 80% saturation) |
| `color.accent.discovery-hover` | `color` | `#280e61` | Discovery accent used by the `discovery` theme. Use it to highlight new features, onboarding and help content. Applied by `help` Messages. (hover, 23% darker) |
| `color.accent.discovery-inactive` | `color` | `#6c4ec9` | Discovery accent used by the `discovery` theme. Use it to highlight new features, onboarding and help content. Applied by `help` Messages. (inactive, 20% brighter) |
| `color.accent.info` | `color` | `var(--color-blue-7)` | Info accent used by the `info` theme. Use it for neutral guidance, tips and system notices. Applied by Alert `info` and `info` Messages and ValidationText. |

| `color.accent.negative` | `color` | `var(--color-red-7)` | Negative accent used by the `negative` theme. Use it for unfavorable values and outcomes, such as a falling metric, where the tone is informational rather than destructive. Applied by Alert `negative`. |

## Ring

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ring.base` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #15151814` | Neutral 3px focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ring`. Container uses it for its focus-visible state, where an offset gap would clash with the surrounding layout. |
| `ring.base-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #15151814` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Exposed as the theme value `ringOffset`. This is the default focus-visible ring for Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader, and the hover/press ring for `ringed` Buttons. |
| `ring.base-subtle` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 1px #15151814` | Neutral 1px hairline focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`) with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtle`. Suited to dense or text-level elements where a 3px ring is too heavy; TableOfContents uses it to mark the focused entry. |

Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans regular at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. ## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
# Cyclone UI (Light Dimmed) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInputField results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Tabs, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup, RadioGroupField and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Ring

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ring.base` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #15151814` | Neutral 3px focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ring`. Container uses it for its focus-visible state, where an offset gap would clash with the surrounding layout. |
| `ring.base-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #15151814` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Exposed as the theme value `ringOffset`. This is the default focus-visible ring for Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader, and the hover/press ring for `ringed` Buttons. |
| `ring.base-subtle` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 1px #15151814` | Neutral 1px hairline focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`) with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtle`. Suited to dense or text-level elements where a 3px ring is too heavy; TableOfContents uses it to mark the focused entry. |
| `ring.base-subtle-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 4px #15151814` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 1px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`). Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtleOffset`. A lighter alternative to `ringOffset` for small or tightly packed controls, such as chips, icon buttons and table cells, where the full ring would crowd neighbours. |
| `ring.brand` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #1fb2a626` | Brand 3px focus ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Resolves from the theme value `ring` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). Use it for focus indication on flush or full-bleed surfaces where an offset gap would look detached. |
| `ring.brand-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #1fb2a626` | Brand focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Resolves from the theme value `ringOffset` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). Focusable controls such as Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader pick it up automatically when rendered in this theme. |
| `ring.brand-subtle` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 1px #1fb2a626` | Brand 1px hairline focus ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`) with no gap. Resolves from the theme value `ringSubtle` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). Suited to dense or text-level elements where a 3px ring is too heavy, such as list items and inline links. |
| `ring.brand-subtle-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 4px #1fb2a626` | Brand focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 1px ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`). Resolves from the theme value `ringSubtleOffset` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). A lighter alternative to `ringOffset` for small or tightly packed controls, such as chips, icon buttons and table cells, where the full ring would crowd neighbours. |
| `ring.danger` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #8e223e26` | Danger 3px focus ring in the danger accent (`accent.danger`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Resolves from the theme value `ring` inside the `danger` theme (used for destructive actions and errors, such as a `danger` Alert or an `error` Message or ValidationText). Use it for focus indication on flush or full-bleed surfaces where an offset gap would look detached. |
| `ring.danger-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #8e223e26` | Danger focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the danger accent (`accent.danger`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Resolves from the theme value `ringOffset` inside the `danger` theme (used for destructive actions and errors, such as a `danger` Alert or an `error` Message or ValidationText). Focusable controls such as Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader pick it up automatically when rendered in this theme. |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans regular at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.button` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Button label style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height so labels center cleanly inside fixed-height controls. Use it for text inside actionable triggers. Used by Button and LinkText. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.display-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small display style: Storm Sans bold at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for subsection headings and dialog or panel titles. HeadingText applies it for level `4`/`sm` (h4). |
| `typography.display-xs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra small display style: Storm Sans semibold at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.display-xxs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra extra small display style: Storm Sans medium at 14px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.eyebrow` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Eyebrow style: Google Sans Code semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height. Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |
| `typography.title-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Large title style: Storm Sans medium at 18px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans regular at 12px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
# Cyclone UI (Light) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInputField results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Tabs, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup, RadioGroupField and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Ring

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ring.base` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #15151814` | Neutral 3px focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ring`. Container uses it for its focus-visible state, where an offset gap would clash with the surrounding layout. |
| `ring.base-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #15151814` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Exposed as the theme value `ringOffset`. This is the default focus-visible ring for Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader, and the hover/press ring for `ringed` Buttons. |
| `ring.base-subtle` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 1px #15151814` | Neutral 1px hairline focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`) with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtle`. Suited to dense or text-level elements where a 3px ring is too heavy; TableOfContents uses it to mark the focused entry. |
| `ring.base-subtle-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 4px #15151814` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 1px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`). Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtleOffset`. A lighter alternative to `ringOffset` for small or tightly packed controls, such as chips, icon buttons and table cells, where the full ring would crowd neighbours. |
| `ring.brand` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #1fb2a626` | Brand 3px focus ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Resolves from the theme value `ring` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). Use it for focus indication on flush or full-bleed surfaces where an offset gap would look detached. |
| `ring.brand-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #1fb2a626` | Brand focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Resolves from the theme value `ringOffset` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). Focusable controls such as Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader pick it up automatically when rendered in this theme. |
| `ring.brand-subtle` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 1px #1fb2a626` | Brand 1px hairline focus ring in the brand accent (`accent.brand`) with no gap. Resolves from the theme value `ringSubtle` inside the `brand` theme (used for primary actions and brand-styled content; Message falls back to this theme when no type is set). ## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans regular at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.button` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Button label style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height so labels center cleanly inside fixed-height controls. Use it for text inside actionable triggers. Used by Button and LinkText. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.display-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small display style: Storm Sans bold at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for subsection headings and dialog or panel titles. HeadingText applies it for level `4`/`sm` (h4). |
| `typography.display-xs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra small display style: Storm Sans semibold at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.display-xxs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra extra small display style: Storm Sans medium at 14px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.eyebrow` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Eyebrow style: Google Sans Code semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height. Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |
| `typography.title-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Large title style: Storm Sans medium at 18px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans regular at 12px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
# Cyclone UI (Dark High Contrast) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInputField results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Tabs, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup, RadioGroupField and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Ring

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ring.base` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #fafafa14` | Neutral 3px focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ring`. Container uses it for its focus-visible state, where an offset gap would clash with the surrounding layout. |
| `ring.base-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #fafafa14` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. Exposed as the theme value `ringOffset`. This is the default focus-visible ring for Button, Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Rating, Badge, Accordion, Dialog, Table, FilePicker and NavigationHeader, and the hover/press ring for `ringed` Buttons. |
| `ring.base-subtle` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 1px #fafafa14` | Neutral 1px hairline focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`) with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtle`. Suited to dense or text-level elements where a 3px ring is too heavy; TableOfContents uses it to mark the focused entry. |
| `ring.base-subtle-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 4px #fafafa14` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 1px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`). Exposed as the theme value `ringSubtleOffset`. ## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans regular at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.button` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Button label style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height so labels center cleanly inside fixed-height controls. Use it for text inside actionable triggers. Used by Button and LinkText. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |

| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans regular at 12px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
# Cyclone UI (Dark Dimmed) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInputField results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Tabs, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup, RadioGroupField and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

| `line-height.none` | `number` | `0.85` | No extra line height (0.85) |
| `line-height.normal` | `number` | `1.375` | Normal line height (1.375) |
| `line-height.relaxed` | `number` | `1.5` | Relaxed line height (1.5) |
| `line-height.sm` | `number` | `1.428571` | Line height for text-sm (calc(1.25 / 0.875)) |
| `line-height.snug` | `number` | `1.25` | Snug line height (1.25) |
| `line-height.tight` | `number` | `1.175` | Tight line height (1.175) |
| `line-height.xl` | `number` | `1.4` | Line height for text-xl (calc(1.75 / 1.25)) |
| `line-height.xs` | `number` | `1.333333` | Line height for text-xs (calc(1 / 0.75)) |

| `ring.base-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #fafafa14` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. ## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans regular at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.button` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Button label style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height so labels center cleanly inside fixed-height controls. Use it for text inside actionable triggers. Used by Button and LinkText. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.display-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small display style: Storm Sans bold at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for subsection headings and dialog or panel titles. HeadingText applies it for level `4`/`sm` (h4). |
| `typography.display-xs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra small display style: Storm Sans semibold at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.display-xxs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra extra small display style: Storm Sans medium at 14px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.eyebrow` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Eyebrow style: Google Sans Code semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height. Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |
| `typography.title-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Large title style: Storm Sans medium at 18px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans regular at 12px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
# Cyclone UI (Dark) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInputField results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Tabs, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup, RadioGroupField and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-md)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

| `ring.base` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px #fafafa14` | Neutral 3px focus ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), drawn flush against the element with no gap. Exposed as the theme value `ring`. Container uses it for its focus-visible state, where an offset gap would clash with the surrounding layout. |
| `ring.base-offset` | `shadow` | `0px 0px 0px 3px var(--color-surface-elevated), 0px 0px 0px 6px #fafafa14` | Neutral focus ring with a 3px gap: an inner band of the elevated surface color followed by a 3px ring in the neutral accent (`accent.base`), so the ring stays legible against the control's own border. ## Text Shadow

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `text-shadow.2xs` | `shadow` | `0px 1px 0px 0px #00000026` | 2X small text shadow |
| `text-shadow.lg` | `shadow` | `0px 1px 2px 0px #0000001a, 0px 3px 2px 0px #0000001a, 0px 4px 8px 0px #0000001a` | Large text shadow |
| `text-shadow.md` | `shadow` | `0px 1px 1px 0px #0000001a, 0px 1px 2px 0px #0000001a, 0px 2px 4px 0px #0000001a` | Medium text shadow |
| `text-shadow.sm` | `shadow` | `0px 1px 0px 0px #00000013, 0px 1px 1px 0px #00000013, 0px 2px 2px 0px #00000013` | Small text shadow |
| `text-shadow.xs` | `shadow` | `0px 1px 1px 0px #00000033` | Extra small text shadow |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.normal}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans regular at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.button` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Button label style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height so labels center cleanly inside fixed-height controls. Use it for text inside actionable triggers. Used by Button and LinkText. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.display-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small display style: Storm Sans bold at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for subsection headings and dialog or panel titles. HeadingText applies it for level `4`/`sm` (h4). |
| `typography.display-xs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra small display style: Storm Sans semibold at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.display-xxs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Extra extra small display style: Storm Sans medium at 14px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for minor headings and compact text elements. HeadingText applies it for level `5`/`xs` (h5). |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.eyebrow` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Eyebrow style: Google Sans Code semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height. Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |
| `typography.title-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Large title style: Storm Sans medium at 18px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans regular at 12px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
